=== PDF第1页 ===
Battery Passport System Data
Push Interface Specification V3.2
Document Nature: This standard defines the unified data push interface specification for 
the Battery Passport System. The fields and formats are fixed and, in principle, no 
customized modifications are accepted.
Disclaimer
1. This specification is unilaterally formulated by the data provider (Battery Passport 
System). Field definitions, data types, and formats are standardized and generally will 
not be adjusted.
2. The receiving party must ensure that their system has the capability to store all fields 
specified in this specification. If data writing fails due to insufficient field length, type 
mismatch, or unsupported enumeration values on the receiving party's side, the 
responsibility shall be borne by the receiving party.
3. If the receiving party confirms that certain fields cannot be adapted, they may propose 
modification requirements. These will be evaluated by the data provider and may be 
supported for a fee, with costs and timelines negotiated separately.
Data Exchange Connection Method Selection
Version
Date
Writer
3.2
2026/9/8
Zheng Naisheng
1. Overview
This document aims to help customers choose the most suitable electronic data 
interchange (API & EDI) connection method based on their technical capabilities, data 
volume, real-time requirements, and security policies. We support the following three 
standard connection protocols:
API (RESTful API, based on HTTPS)
OFTP (Odette File Transfer Protocol, including OFTP2)
AS2 (Applicability Statement 2)
○
○
○
2. Method 1: API (RESTful) – Information Required from the Receiving Party
Under the API method, the receiving party provides the interface address and 
authorization credentials, and we act as the client to call it.

*   2.2 **Our Request Specification**
    | Item | Description |
    | :--- | :--- |
    | Request Method | POST |
    | Data Format | `application/json; charset=utf-8` |
    | Character Encoding | UTF-8 |
    | Timeout | We set it to ≥ 30 seconds. It is recommended that the 
receiving party's processing time be controlled within 25 seconds. |
    | Rate Limit | Defined by the receiving party. A minimum of 60 
calls per minute is recommended. |
*   2.3 **Request Header (Header) Specification (Fixed and Mandatory)**
    `Content-Type: application/json`
    `Accept: application/json`
    `X-System: BatteryPassport` (Our fixed identifier)
    `X-API-Version: v2.0` (Interface version)
    `Authorization: Bearer {access_token}` (Issued by the receiving 
party)
    **Header Field Description:**

*   **Authorization Method Explanation**:
    We will obtain the `access_token` from the receiving party's 
authentication service via the OAuth2 Client Credentials flow.
    Token validity period is recommended to be ≥ 1 hour. We will 
automatically refresh it upon expiration.
    All requests are transmitted via TLS 1.2+.

}

Type
Description
code ﻿
integer
Status code, 0 indicates 
success, non-0 indicates 
failure.

string

data ﻿
object
Response data, contains 
batch number and receipt 
time on success.
